## 2026-10-26 eGFR handoff (not released)

HealthTrend main adds a display-only adult CKD-EPI 2021 eGFR series from exact CREA values plus profile birth date and sex. The derived key is EGFR-CKDEPI2021; point clicks resolve to the original CREA LabResult. Tests cover formula values, units, age-at-test, invalid inputs, full Trends card controls, and non-mutation. Await user UI confirmation before release.
